do\n  bytes_file=$(mktemp)\n  t1=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  for i in $(seq 1 $n); do\n    curl -o /dev/null -s -w \"%{size_download}\\n\" --max-time 25 \"$URL\" >> $bytes_file &\n  done\n  wait\n  t2=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  total=$(awk \"{sum+=\\$1} END{print sum}\" $bytes_file)\n  elapsed=$(awk -v a=$t1 -v b=$t2 \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.2f\\\", b-a}\")\n  mbps=$(awk -v t=$total -v e=$elapsed \"BEGIN{if(e>0)printf \\\"%.0f\\\", t*8/1e6/e; else print 0}\")\n  printf \"  aliyun %2d streams: %4d MB in %5s s = %4s Mbps  (per-stream avg %d Mbps)\\n\" $n $((total/1048576)) $elapsed $mbps $((mbps/n + 0))\n  rm -f $bytes_file\ndone\n\necho\necho \"===== Upload test to aliyun via Cloudflare worker or similar =====\"\nhead -c 41943040 /dev/urandom > /tmp/u.bin\necho \"-- 40MB upload via Cloudflare worker --\"\ncurl -o /dev/null -s -w \"UL speed: %{speed_upload} B/s = %{size_upload}B in %{time_total}s\\n\" --max-time 25 -F \"f=@/tmp/u.bin\" https://speed.cloudflare.com/__up\necho \"-- 40MB upload via Aliyun (no easy endpoint, try OSS test) --\"\n# fallback: just test by uploading to any echo endpoint\ncurl -o /dev/null -s -w \"UL speed: %{speed_upload} B/s\\n\" --max-time 25 -X POST --data-binary @/tmp/u.bin https://httpbin.org/post 2>&1\nrm /tmp/u.bin' 2>&1",

do\n  sysctl -w net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control=$cc > /dev/null 2>&1\n  sleep 1\n  total=0\n  for i in 1 2 3; do\n    v=$(curl -o /dev/null -s -w \"%{speed_download}\" --max-time 12 \"$URL\")\n    total=$(awk -v a=$total -v b=$v \"BEGIN{print a+b}\")\n  done\n  avg=$(awk -v t=$total \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.0f\\\", t/3}\")\n  mbps=$(awk -v a=$avg \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.0f\\\", a*8/1e6}\")\n  printf \"  %-6s : avg %s B/s = %s Mbps\\n\" $cc $avg $mbps\ndone\nsysctl -w net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control=bbr > /dev/null\n\necho\necho \"===== Check if CERNET education mirror (USTB) is fast (lower RTT) =====\"\nfor url in \\\n  \"https://mirrors.ustc.edu.cn/debian/dists/bookworm/main/Contents-amd64.gz\" \\\n  \"https://mirror.nju.edu.cn/debian/dists/bookworm/main/Contents-amd64.gz\" \\\n  \"https://mirrors.bfsu.edu.cn/debian/dists/bookworm/main/Contents-amd64.gz\" \\\n  \"https://mirrors.sjtug.sjtu.edu.cn/debian/dists/bookworm/main/Contents-amd64.gz\" \\\n; do\n  host=$(echo $url | sed -nE \"s,https://([^/]+)/.*,\\1,p\")\n  # parallel 8 streams\n  bytes_file=$(mktemp); t1=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8; do\n    curl -o /dev/null -s -w \"%{size_download}\\n\" --max-time 12 \"$url\" >> $bytes_file &\n  done\n  wait\n  t2=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  total=$(awk \"{s+=\\$1} END{print s}\" $bytes_file)\n  elapsed=$(awk -v a=$t1 -v b=$t2 \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.2f\\\", b-a}\")\n  mbps=$(awk -v t=$total -v e=$elapsed \"BEGIN{if(e>0)printf \\\"%.0f\\\", t*8/1e6/e; else print 0}\")\n  printf \"  %-35s 8-stream: %s MB in %s s = %s Mbps\\n\" \"$host\" $((total/1048576)) $elapsed $mbps\n  rm -f $bytes_file\ndone\n\necho\necho \"===== install aria2 from CN package mirror if available =====\"\n# OpenWrt official: aria2 exists\nopkg list | grep -E \"^aria2\" | head -3\n# try alternative source\nopkg install aria2 luci-app-aria2 2>&1 | tail -3 || true\n\necho\necho \"===== one-shot bandwidth: speedtest-go full output =====\"\n./speedtest-go --multi --thread 16 2>&1 | tail -10' 2>&1",

do\n  h=$(echo $url | sed -nE \"s,https://([^/]+)/.*,\\1,p\")\n  # range request first 50MB\n  printf \"  %-35s \" \"$h\"\n  curl -o /dev/null -s -r 0-52428799 -w \"code=%{http_code} size=%{size_download} speed=%{speed_download} time=%{time_total}\\n\" --max-time 12 \"$url\"\ndone\n\necho\necho \"===== Find any HUST-internal large file =====\"\n# HUST mirror direct large iso\nfor url in \\\n  \"https://mirror.hust.edu.cn/ubuntu-releases/24.04/ubuntu-24.04.1-desktop-amd64.iso\" \\\n  \"http://mirror.hust.edu.cn/ubuntu-releases/24.04/ubuntu-24.04.1-desktop-amd64.iso\" \\\n  \"https://mirrors.hust.edu.cn/anaconda/archive/Anaconda3-2024.10-1-Linux-x86_64.sh\" \\\n; do\n  h=$(echo $url | sed -nE \"s,https?://([^/]+)/.*,\\1,p\")\n  printf \"  %-35s \" \"$h\"\n  curl -o /dev/null -s -r 0-52428799 -w \"code=%{http_code} size=%{size_download} speed=%{speed_download}\\n\" --max-time 12 -L \"$url\"\ndone\n\necho\necho \"===== Single-stream curl on working URL (3 runs) =====\"\n# Use one we know works from speedtest path or aliyun\nURL=\"https://mirrors.aliyun.com/centos/7.9.2009/isos/x86_64/CentOS-7-x86_64-Minimal-2009.iso\"\nfor i in 1 2 3; do\n  v=$(curl -o /dev/null -s -r 0-52428799 -w \"%{speed_download}\" --max-time 12 \"$URL\")\n  mbps=$(awk -v a=$v \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.0f\\\", a*8/1e6}\")\n  printf \"  run$i: %s B/s = %s Mbps\\n\" $v $mbps\ndone\n\necho\necho \"===== Revert initcwnd (might hurt aggressive CN paths) =====\"\nip route change default via 10.17.43.254 dev wan proto static src 10.17.43.78 2>&1\nip route show default\necho\necho \"===== Aliyun parallel reconfirm (best CN path) =====\"\nURL=\"https://mirrors.aliyun.com/centos/7.9.2009/isos/x86_64/CentOS-7-x86_64-Minimal-2009.iso\"\nfor n in 1 4 8; do\n  bytes_file=$(mktemp); t1=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  for i in $(seq 1 $n); do\n    curl -o /dev/null -s -r 0-20971519 -w \"%{size_download}\\n\" --max-time 15 \"$URL\" >> $bytes_file &\n  done\n  wait\n  t2=$(awk \"{print \\$1}\" /proc/uptime)\n  total=$(awk \"{s+=\\$1} END{print s}\" $bytes_file)\n  elapsed=$(awk -v a=$t1 -v b=$t2 \"BEGIN{printf \\\"%.2f\\\", b-a}\")\n  mbps=$(awk -v t=$total -v e=$elapsed \"BEGIN{if(e>0)printf \\\"%.0f\\\", t*8/1e6/e}\")\n  printf \"  aliyun-iso %2d parallel: %s MB in %s s = %s Mbps\\n\" $n $((total/1048576)) $elapsed $mbps\n  rm -f $bytes_file\ndone' 2>&1",
```json
  "description": "Working CN URLs + clean single-stream + parallel"
}
```

> AGENT

实测结果出来了，结论清晰：

## 🎯 真相

| 测量方式 | 实际带宽 |
|---|---|
| **speedtest-go 8 线程 → CERNET 内** | **DL 447-511 Mbps / UL 160-180 Mbps** |
| **多流 → 阿里云 CDN** | ~125 Mbps（阿里端限速）|
| **单流 → USTC 镜像** | 30-40 Mbps（USTC 端限速）|
| **单流 → 阿里云** | 50 Mbps（阿里端限速）|

**关键观察**：

1. **校园 WAN 真实容量 = 500 / 180 Mbps**（speedtest 三次稳定证实）——之前我以为的 92/57 是被远端服务器的 per-IP 限速骗了。

2. **单流 30-50 Mbps 是远端限速，不是路由器问题**：
   - 改 BBR/cubic/reno 都一样（USTC 测试三种 CC 都是 37 Mbps）
   - 同一台 USTC 8 流比 1 流还慢（25 vs 33 Mbps）——证实是 per-source 限速

3. **路由器 CPU 没瓶颈**：30MB 下载触发 3 ticks softirq, 0 ksoftirqd, 1 WAN drop。

## 🛠 路由器侧已经榨干

- WAN qdisc：mq + fq_codel ✓
- TCP 缓冲 8MB ✓
- BBR ✓
- 多核 RPS + IRQ 分核 ✓
- Fullcone NAT ✓
- 无 SQM 拖累 ✓
- conntrack 充足 ✓

**没有任何路由器配置能突破远端服务器的 per-source 限速**。

## 💡 用户端真正能加速的办法

| 场景 | 解决 |
|---|---|
| 下大文件 | 用 **aria2 / IDM / Motrix**（多源 + 多线程下载器）|
| 浏览器下载 | Chrome 启用「并行下载」（chrome://flags/#enable-parallel-downloading）|
| Linux/Mac 命令行 | `aria2c -x 8 -s 8 -j 4 <url>` 或 `axel -n 8` |
| apt / yum 镜像 | 配多 mirror，包管理器会并发 |
| 看到 50 Mbps 烦人 | 配高速镜像源（清华/中科大同时配）|

要不要我把这些工具用法整理一份贴在 TUNING.md 后面？另外，**你用什么设备/什么场景觉得慢？** 如果具体到某个 app（比如 Mac 下 `brew install`、Steam 下载、某网站慢），我可以针对性调（比如改 hosts、给那个 app 配多镜像、调它的并发度）。
